([phabdev/game-quality-gate](https://github.com/phabdev/game-quality-gate/blob/HEAD/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Mechanical-verification sub-agent for the .H (and optionally .RR) pipeline. Reopens the SAVED workbook fresh, read-only, and recomputes every deterministic gate — tie-out sums, SpecialCells formula-error scan, Check-row read-backs, census 12-month coverage, byte-compare join samples — and returns the raw evidence table. NO judgment, NO interpretation, NO pass/fail verdicts on anything requiring a ruling: it reports numbers; h-verifier (a stronger, different model) audits them. Spawned by the h-underwrite orchestrator after assembly + save; not for direct use. ([RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting](https://github.com/RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs the project's release gates (lint, test, build, bundle:check) and returns ONLY a concise pass/fail summary plus the failing output. ([salihoglueyup/EcozyonTech](https://github.com/salihoglueyup/EcozyonTech/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs the full deterministic gate and returns only the failures. Use to verify a slice without polluting the main context with build output. ([seulnan/tomolog-server](https://github.com/seulnan/tomolog-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs the gates a brief names (backend and frontend tests, lint, E2E, the API-client staleness check, the hook cases) and reports their real output. Fixes nothing. ([tarka1939/My_Site](https://github.com/tarka1939/My_Site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gate-runner.md))
- **gate-runner**: Runs one trevokeyless gate set (rust, python, ts, scripts, or cross) — the local CI-equivalent commands — and returns a compact pass/fail report. - **gate-runner**: Mechanical first tier of agent supervision. Never judges the diff, never writes a verdict, never edits files. Invoke from the agent-supervisor skill on every READY_FOR_REVIEW or new commit. - **gate-runner**: Run this repository's full CI gate — both the Python/docs half and the frontend half — and report a per-command exit-code table with only the failing excerpt. - **gd-fiche**: Membre de l'équipe Game Design (chef game-design) — fiche de personnage et toutes les règles qui la font fonctionner — création, caractéristiques et croix, compétences, acquis et plafonds, entraînement, niveaux et gains, maîtrises, métiers, sorts sur la fiche, équipement porté, contrôles, fiche auto V15.2.2. Analyse une règle de son domaine, ses sources, ses décisions et son impact sur le moteur. Lancé par game-design avec un brief. ([Bravexier/MaverickCenter](https://github.com/Bravexier/MaverickCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-fiche.md))
- **gd-keeper**: Хранитель управляющей идеи и аудитор связности — после каждой закрытой стадии переаудитит все предыдущие на дрейф от управляющей идеи и внутренние противоречия, пишет CONSISTENCY.md и может вернуть BLOCK. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-keeper.md))
- **gd-magies**: Membre de l'équipe Game Design (chef game-design) — magies et sorts — sorts, coûts, portées, effets, niveaux de maîtrise (NM), puissance magique (NPM), résistances entre magies, invocations et créatures invoquées. Analyse une règle de son domaine, ses sources, ses décisions et son impact. Lancé par game-design avec un brief. ([Bravexier/MaverickCenter](https://github.com/Bravexier/MaverickCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-magies.md))
- **gd-mecaniques**: Membre de l'équipe Game Design (chef game-design) — mécaniques de jeu — combat (jets, dégâts, défense AP/AM, dégâts reçus, deux armes), postures, Mode Hyper et Mode Super, altérations d'état, paliers de résistance, météo, maladies, règles spécifiques des pages du guide, vocabulaire des règles (glossaire). Analyse une règle de son domaine, ses sources, ses décisions et son impact. Lancé par game-design avec un brief. ([Bravexier/MaverickCenter](https://github.com/Bravexier/MaverickCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-mecaniques.md))
- **gd-mechanics**: Проектирует ядро игры — core loop, пространство/объекты/действия/правила, цели и значимые выборы — через линзы механик Шелла и дописывает раздел «Механики» в GDD.md. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-mechanics.md))
- **gd-narrative**: Проектирует историю и мир игры — нарратив, мир, аватар, персонажей и story-machine — по линзам Шелла стадии 5, дописывает раздел «Нарратив и мир» в GDD.md и фиксирует ответы линз в LENS-LOG.md. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-narrative.md))
- **gd-objets**: Membre de l'équipe Game Design (chef game-design) — objets — armes (forge, matériaux, améliorations, durabilité, portée), armures et boucliers, accessoires (générateur, malédictions), consommables (potions, huiles), bâtons magiques, composants, artisanat et recettes, monnaies. Analyse une règle de son domaine, ses sources, ses décisions et son impact. Lancé par game-design avec un brief. ([Bravexier/MaverickCenter](https://github.com/Bravexier/MaverickCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-objets.md))
- **gd-playtest**: Готовит план плейтеста и итераций — что проверяем, на ком, какие риски, как крутим петлю (Rule of the Loop) — и дописывает раздел «Плейтест» в GDD.md, фиксируя ответы линз в LENS-LOG.md. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-playtest.md))
- **gd-prototyper**: Дизайнер зонда — формулирует ОДИН рискованный вопрос, выбирает самую лёгкую форму по лестнице (таблица/бумага/ASCII/веб) и пишет PROTOTYPE-PROBE.md с рассуждением. Не кодит и не запускает. Передаёт дизайн + мини-ТЗ оркестратору; тот зовёт gd-builder для сборки и прогона. Контракт OK | BLOCK. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-prototyper.md))
- **gd-races**: Membre de l'équipe Game Design (chef game-design) — races, ethnies, peuples, magies innées et accessibles, dons de race, Mode Hyper de race ; combinaisons possibles race × classe × magie (races pures / hybrides, classes réservées ou fermées, mono-magie). Analyse une règle de son domaine, ses sources, ses décisions et son impact. Lancé par game-design avec un brief. ([Bravexier/MaverickCenter](https://github.com/Bravexier/MaverickCenter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-races.md))
- **gd-release**: Стадия 7 пайплайна — готовит релиз и бизнес игры (питч, комьюнити, монетизация, ответственность, тех-/деплой-план) через линзы Pitch/Profit/Transformation/Responsibility/Community/Griefing; пишет PITCH.md и раздел «Релиз» в GDD.md, ответы складывает в LENS-LOG.md. ([Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck](https://github.com/Goryuchnick/schell-lens-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd-release.md))
- **gd-sojin**: Park So-jin (박소진) · Girl's Day · Leader / Lead Vocal · 个性: 2.5代队长·御姐·甜美 · UI: 期待 anticipation copy ([SuanFishXYY/kpop-design-system](https://github.com/SuanFishXYY/kpop-design-system/blob/HEAD/agents/gd-sojin.md) · ★1)
- **gd_difficulty** ([merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N](https://github.com/merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd_difficulty.md))

- **gd_faction_dynamics** ([merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N](https://github.com/merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd_faction_dynamics.md))

- **gd_onboarding** ([merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N](https://github.com/merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd_onboarding.md))
- **gd_pacing** ([merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N](https://github.com/merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd_pacing.md))
- **gd_reward_loop** ([merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N](https://github.com/merlingame-netizen/M.E.R.L.I.N/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gd_reward_loop.md))
- **gdbox-acceptor**: The independent acceptance tester of a GDBox hybrid team. Use when you are the producer and the game is ready to deliver. In the prompt, give the team id, your delivery report (what was built) and how to play it (start scene, controls, win and lose conditions). Do not use to build or fix anything, or outside a GDBox team. ([ueboxai/gdbox](https://github.com/ueboxai/gdbox/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/claude-plugin-hybrid/agents/gdbox-acceptor.md) · ★110)
- **gdbox-acceptor**: The independent acceptance tester of a GDBox studio team. Use when you are the producer of a GDBox external team and the game is ready to deliver. In the prompt, give the team id, your delivery report (what was built) and how to play it (start scene, controls, win and lose conditions). Do not use to build or fix anything, or outside a GDBox team. ([ueboxai/gdbox](https://github.com/ueboxai/gdbox/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/claude-plugin/agents/gdbox-acceptor.md) · ★110)
- **gdbox-member**: A teammate in a GDBox studio team. Use when you are the producer of a GDBox external team (after team_open) and want a teammate you registered with team_hire to do one assignment. In the prompt, give the team id, the teammate's name exactly as hired, and the assignment. Do not use for acceptance testing (use gdbox-acceptor) or outside a GDBox team. ([ueboxai/gdbox](https://github.com/ueboxai/gdbox/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/claude-plugin/agents/gdbox-member.md) · ★110)
- **gdc-analyst**: Analyzes GDC graph and provides architecture insights without making changes ([ulgerang/gdc](https://github.com/ulgerang/gdc/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/gdc-analyst.md))
- **gdc-build**: GDC-aware implementation agent - implements code from YAML specifications ([ulgerang/gdc](https://github.com/ulgerang/gdc/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/gdc-build.md))
- **gdc-discovery**: Use when looking for cohorts or files in NCI GDC (TCGA, TARGET, CPTAC, etc.). Queries the live API by project, tissue, data type, experimental strategy, workflow, access level and patient list, and reports file count, total size and case count with a manifest. Does not download or submit. ([Sejung98/Jige](https://github.com/Sejung98/Jige/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gdc-discovery.md))
- **gdd**: Damocles GDD Architect. Designs, maintains, and evolves the Game Design Document — the authoritative constitution for all Damocles content. Iteratively interviews the user via the ask-questions tool to author new design sections, review existing rules, resolve contradictions, audit tonal consistency, design new content types, refine mechanical frameworks, and onboard new contributors. The GDD is NOT a wiki — it is a constitution. Use when: designing new GDD sections, reviewing design rules, resolving lore/mechanical contradictions, creating new content type templates, auditing tonal/thematic… ([Typeir/Ikuisuus](https://github.com/Typeir/Ikuisuus/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/gdd.agent.md))
- **gdd-accessibility-checker**: Validates designs against WCAG AA accessibility standards. Checks contrast ratios, touch targets, and semantic structure. Spawned by /gdd:audit-accessibility. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-accessibility-checker.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-analyst**: General module executor — competitor profiles, moat analysis, customer evidence. Spawned by module commands with a module brief. ([Navis-Advisory/gdd](https://github.com/Navis-Advisory/gdd/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-analyst.md) · 2 copies)
- **gdd-architect**: Interviews the user about game design and writes or restructures the documents in docs/design/ — pillars, core loop, systems, biomes. Use when starting design work from scratch (including a brand-new game from this template), when the design needs to be pinned down before code, or when the design documents have drifted out of shape. Not for routine edits to a single document. ([ElCalabozoDelDev/DungeonEngine](https://github.com/ElCalabozoDelDev/DungeonEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gdd-architect.md))
- **gdd-auditor**: Independent GDD reviewer. Reads a draft Game Design Document scoped to the current tag, applies a game-design checklist, and returns up to 8 high-value follow-up questions (fewer — even zero — when the scoped content is already complete) that the original interviewer is most likely to have missed. Read-only — MUST NOT modify the GDD or any other file. ([RandallLiuXin/GodotMaker](https://github.com/RandallLiuXin/GodotMaker/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-auditor.md) · ★563)
- **gdd-auditor**: GDD pipeline agent that audits a drafted GDD.md against the interview ledger, the family template, and the best-practices checklist. Verifies traceability (every normative claim maps to a decided or assumed ledger row or is labeled an assumption), completeness, pillar consistency, and anti-pattern avoidance. Read-only on the GDD; writes gdd-audit.md for the orchestrator. ([MisterVitoPro/gdd](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/gdd/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **gdd-brief-extractor**: Synthesizes user answers from the questioning phase into a structured BRIEF.md. Spawned by /gdd:new-design orchestrator. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-brief-extractor.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-build**: Builds one GDD change through its gates. The GDD orchestrator launches it with the brief and the seal of its checks. ([MarsGotta/gdd](https://github.com/MarsGotta/gdd/blob/HEAD/skills/adapters/claude-code/agents/gdd-build.md))
- **gdd-checks**: Writes and seals the cases of a GDD change, a test for each scenario of its brief, or sets up a project's standing checks. The GDD orchestrator launches it. It never builds the change it writes cases for. ([MarsGotta/gdd](https://github.com/MarsGotta/gdd/blob/HEAD/skills/adapters/claude-code/agents/gdd-checks.md))
- **gdd-code-simplifier**: 直近の差分を対象に、振る舞いを保ったまま自動で簡素化する。GDD の Step 14 REFACTOR チェックポイントから dispatch される。 &lt;example&gt; Context: GREEN 後に Step 14 REFACTOR として自動 dispatch される user: "Step 14 の簡素化パスを実行して" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-code-simplifier agent to simplify the diff without changing behavior." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 実装差分の重複・過剰ネスト・過剰抽象を整理したい user: "この実装、振る舞いを変えずに読みやすくして" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-code-simplifier agent to simplify without altering behavior." &lt;/example&gt; ([yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development](https://github.com/yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development/blob/HEAD/plugins/gate-driven-development/agents/gdd-code-simplifier.md))
- **gdd-completion-reviewer**: Final comprehensive review before completion. Checks full diff, steering artifacts, reviewer selection records, verification evidence, cross-cutting risks, and advisory code quality. Use inside `gdd-completion-check`. &lt;example&gt; Context: 全 task 完了後に最後の横断レビューを入れたい user: "最後に包括レビューを入れて" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-completion-reviewer agent during gdd-completion-check for a final cross-cutting and code quality review." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: done 前に全体の整合性とコード品質を確認したい user: "完了前に全体をもう一度見て" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-completion-reviewer agent to check cross-task correctness, workflow inte… ([yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development](https://github.com/yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development/blob/HEAD/plugins/gate-driven-development/agents/gdd-completion-reviewer.md))
- **gdd-concept**: Writes concept.md and decisions.md for a gdd-forge v2 GDD from a pitch and the user's answers. Dispatched by the /gdd-forge:concept skill; not for ad-hoc use. ([eternalTornado/gdd-forge](https://github.com/eternalTornado/gdd-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-concept.md))
- **gdd-critic**: The adversarial GDD critic — first line of defense. A hyper-critical reviewer that screens every batch of agent-generated content against the GDD before qa-validator, catching and correcting scope creep, contradiction, ungrounded invention, and tone drift. Use after any content worker produces a batch. Flags and corrects; never approves its own pass or writes game content. ([BakerDad/BA_Demo](https://github.com/BakerDad/BA_Demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gdd-critic.md))
- **gdd-design-critic**: Reviews rendered designs for quality, consistency, and accessibility. Produces structured critiques with severity ratings. Spawned by /gdd:critique-phase. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-design-critic.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-design-researcher**: Researches UI patterns, competitor designs, and current trends for a product domain. Spawned by /gdd:new-design or /gdd:explore-phase. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-design-researcher.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-design-roadmapper**: Creates design phase roadmaps from briefs and requirements. Maps DES-IDs to phases with success criteria. Spawned by /gdd:new-design orchestrator. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-design-roadmapper.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-design-specifier**: Creates detailed design specifications for a phase, mapping every visual element to design tokens. Spawned by /gdd:spec-phase. ([0martinez/get-design-done](https://github.com/0martinez/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-design-specifier.md) · ★5)
- **gdd-designer**: Creates and maintains the Game Design Document and player-facing intent. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/agents/gdd-designer.md) · ★110)
- **gdd-fable-advisor**: GDD の判断の相談役（Fable・medium）。references/human-check-template.md § 事前回答の委任先が fable のとき（references/autonomy.md 判断の手順）、利用する repo の規則がこの agent を名指ししたときに、その手順から起動する。ユーザーが自分のターンで Fable の意見を名指しで求めたとき（「Fable の意見を聞きたい」「Fable に聞いて」など）も、その相談に 1 回起動する（その確認の材料を場所で渡す）。それ以外のユーザーの依頼から推し量って起動しない。役は、欠陥を先に見つけることではなく、ユーザーならどの案を選ぶかの代わりである。起動時に model を渡さない。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 本人が判断を委任した human check で、委任先が fable のとき（references/autonomy.md 判断の手順 1） user: "(references/autonomy.md 判断の手順 1 の dispatch。人間の自由発話ではない)" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-fable-advisor agent once, as references/autonomy.md step 1 specifies." &lt;/example… ([yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development](https://github.com/yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development/blob/HEAD/plugins/gate-driven-development/agents/gdd-fable-advisor.md))

- **gdd-formal-gate-runner**: GDD の formal gate phase（planning・design-gating・implementation-gating・completion-check）を 1 回実行する。references/subagent-dispatch.md の Template からだけ起動し、ユーザーの依頼から直接は起動しない。 &lt;example&gt; Context: /gdd-dev instruction 10 STEP 2 が、承認後に Design-Gating Template を Read・置換して dispatch する（人間の自由発話からの直接起動ではない） user: "(/gdd-dev 内部の Template 置換 dispatch。人間の自由発話ではない)" assistant: "I'll use the gdd-formal-gate-runner agent with the design-gating Template in references/subagent-dispatch.md, exactly as commands/gdd-dev.md instruction 10 STEP 2 specifies." &lt;/example&gt; ([yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development](https://github.com/yamaguchi-tk/gate-driven-development/blob/HEAD/plugins/gate-driven-development/agents/gdd-formal-gate-runner.md))
- **gdd-graph-refresh**: Refreshes the knowledge graph at .design/graph/graph.json from .design/intel/ slices using the native bin/gdd-graph CLI. - **gdscript-performance**: GDScript is an interpreter in front of a C++ engine. The expensive work is not usually the `draw_*` call - it is the per-element loop that builds the arguments, the node construction around it, the allocation per cell, and the cross-FFI call per item. This page is the method for finding and proving one of those costs, and the rule for deciding whether the work belongs in Rust. ([godot-pty/gpty](https://github.com/godot-pty/gpty/blob/HEAD/docs/content/docs/agents/gdscript-performance.md) · ★78)
